<h2 id="overview" class="group">Overview<span class="relative hidden h-[1em] w-0 align-middle lg:inline-block"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" data-cds-icon-only="" data-cds-ghost="" data-size="xs" class="cds-reset group/btn isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none cursor-[var(--cds-cursor-interactive)] aria-disabled:cursor-default data-[disabled]:cursor-default border-0 outline-none focus-visible:outline-hidden [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:outline-hidden rounded h-control font-sans text-body [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-disabled disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast text-primary font-normal font-control aria-pressed:text-accent focus-visible:shadow-focus [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:shadow-focus aspect-square w-control px-0 absolute top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus-visible:opacity-100" aria-label="Copy link to clipboard" id="_R_h4qav5ul5u9febtdpl4plb_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] inset-0 cds-btn-squish"><span data-cds-part="paint" class="absolute inset-0 rounded-[inherit] transition-[background-color,box-shadow,color] duration-fast ease-out group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] group-[[data-initial-focus]:focus]/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent "></span></span><span class="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:533.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button></span></h2>
<div class="prose-block"><p>Claude Fable 5.1 extends Claude Fable 5 at the same input and output prices, with cache reads at a quarter of the cost, and brings stronger long-running agentic coding, multistep research, and document, spreadsheet, and slide work. For most workloads, start with Claude Opus 5.5 (see <a href="/docs/en/about-claude/models/choosing-a-model">Choosing a model</a>). Use Claude Fable 5.1 for demanding reasoning and long-horizon agentic work, or when your evals on Claude Opus 5.5 at higher effort still fall short. Claude Mythos 5.1 offers the same capabilities only to organizations verified through Anthropic&#x27;s verification programs, such as the <a href="https://support.claude.com/en/articles/14604842" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Cyber Verification Program</a>.</p><p>If you already call Claude Fable 5, three changes are breaking: <a href="/docs/en/models/fable-5-1/whats-new-fable-5-1#forced-tool-use-is-not-supported">forced tool use returns an error</a>, <a href="/docs/en/models/fable-5-1/whats-new-fable-5-1#thinking-blocks-are-tied-to-the-model-that-produced-them">only Claude Fable 5.1 and Claude Mythos 5.1 can read Claude Fable 5.1&#x27;s thinking blocks</a>, and <a href="/docs/en/models/fable-5-1/whats-new-fable-5-1#editing-earlier-turns-invalidates-thinking-blocks">editing earlier turns invalidates thinking blocks</a>. Five are additive: <a href="/docs/en/models/fable-5-1/whats-new-fable-5-1#change-effort-mid-conversation-beta">per-message effort</a> (beta), <a href="/docs/en/models/fable-5-1/whats-new-fable-5-1#turn-scoped-system-messages-beta">turn-scoped system messages</a> (beta), <a href="/docs/en/models/fable-5-1/whats-new-fable-5-1#progress-updates-between-tool-calls-beta">readable progress updates between tool calls</a> (<code>display: &quot;updates&quot;</code>, beta), a <a href="/docs/en/models/fable-5-1/whats-new-fable-5-1#pricing">lower cache read price</a>, and <a href="/docs/en/models/fable-5-1/whats-new-fable-5-1#content-provenance">content provenance</a>.</p><p><a class="cds-reset cds-text-link inline-flex items-baseline gap-[0.25em] cursor-pointer" data-cds="TextLink" href="/docs/en/models/fable-5-1/whats-new-fable-5-1"><span class="cds-text-link-underline">What&#x27;s new in Claude Fable 5.1</span><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:1lh;font-weight:533.3" class="self-center" aria-hidden="true"></span></a></p></div>
<h2 id="claude-fable-5-1-and-claude-mythos-5-1" class="group">Claude Fable 5.1 and Claude Mythos 5.1<span class="relative hidden h-[1em] w-0 align-middle lg:inline-block"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" data-cds-icon-only="" data-cds-ghost="" data-size="xs" class="cds-reset group/btn isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none cursor-[var(--cds-cursor-interactive)] aria-disabled:cursor-default data-[disabled]:cursor-default border-0 outline-none focus-visible:outline-hidden [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:outline-hidden rounded h-control font-sans text-body [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-disabled disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast text-primary font-normal font-control aria-pressed:text-accent focus-visible:shadow-focus [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:shadow-focus aspect-square w-control px-0 absolute top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus-visible:opacity-100" aria-label="Copy link to clipboard" id="_R_i4qav5ul5u9febtdpl4plb_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] inset-0 cds-btn-squish"><span data-cds-part="paint" class="absolute inset-0 rounded-[inherit] transition-[background-color,box-shadow,color] duration-fast ease-out group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] group-[[data-initial-focus]:focus]/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent "></span></span><span class="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:533.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button></span></h2>
<p><a href="/docs/en/models/mythos-5-1/overview">Claude Mythos 5.1</a> offers the same capabilities only to organizations verified through Anthropic&#x27;s verification programs, such as the <a href="https://support.claude.com/en/articles/14604842" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Cyber Verification Program</a>. It shares Claude Fable 5.1&#x27;s specifications and pricing. To request access, apply to the program that covers your use case, or contact your Anthropic, AWS, or Google Cloud account team.</p>
<h2 id="how-it-compares" class="group">How it compares<span class="relative hidden h-[1em] w-0 align-middle lg:inline-block"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" data-cds-icon-only="" data-cds-ghost="" data-size="xs" class="cds-reset group/btn isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none cursor-[var(--cds-cursor-interactive)] aria-disabled:cursor-default data-[disabled]:cursor-default border-0 outline-none focus-visible:outline-hidden [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:outline-hidden rounded h-control font-sans text-body [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-disabled disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast text-primary font-normal font-control aria-pressed:text-accent focus-visible:shadow-focus [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:shadow-focus aspect-square w-control px-0 absolute top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus-visible:opacity-100" aria-label="Copy link to clipboard" id="_R_j4qav5ul5u9febtdpl4plb_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] inset-0 cds-btn-squish"><span data-cds-part="paint" class="absolute inset-0 rounded-[inherit] transition-[background-color,box-shadow,color] duration-fast ease-out group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] group-[[data-initial-focus]:focus]/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent "></span></span><span class="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:533.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button></span></h2>
